    VAR
      WebshopWebforms@1000000002 : Codeunit 5447306;
      WebshopManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000001 : Codeunit 5447301;
      YPos@1000000005 : Integer;
      YPosSettings@1000000009 : Integer;
      Spacer@1000000006 : Integer;
      HeightAmazon@1000000007 : Integer;
      HeightEbay@1000000008 : Integer;
      HeightMarketplaceType@1000000021 : Integer;
      EbayAuthToken@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      EbayAuthTokenBigText@1000000010 : BigText;
      OStream@1000000011 : OutStream;
      IStream@1000000012 : InStream;
      CustomerLinkVisible@1000000013 : Boolean;
      SalespersonLinkVisible@1000000003 : Boolean;
      NoSeries@1000000020 : Record 308;
      UseOnlineOrderNoPossible@1000000019 : Boolean;
      UseOnlineOrderNoError@1000000018 : Text[1024];
      SalesSetup@1000000017 : Record 311;
      AmazonFieldsVisible@1000000016 : Boolean;
      EbayFieldsVisible@1000000015 : Boolean;
      MarketplaceVisible@1000000014 : Boolean;
      Text001@1000000028 : TextConst 'ENU=Biite aktivieren Sie "Manuelle Nr." in der Nummernserie "%1".';
      Text002@1000000027 : TextConst 'ENU=Nummernserie "%1" nicht gefunden.';
      Text003@1000000026 : TextConst 'ENU=Biite aktivieren Sie "Manuelle Nr." in der Nummernserie "%1".';
      Text004@1000000025 : TextConst 'ENU=Keine Standardnummernserie definiert.';
      Text007@1000000024 : TextConst 'DEU=Bitte zuerst das n�tige Steuerelement installieren.;ENU=Please install needed Client AddIn first';
      Text008@1000000023 : TextConst 'DEU=Anfrage wird verarbeitet...;ENU=Request is processed';
      Text009@1000000022 : TextConst 'DEU=Kategorien werden abgeholt;ENU=Categories are being fetched';

    PROCEDURE SetControlsVisible@1000000006();
    BEGIN
      YPos := 990;
      YPosSettings := 0;
      Spacer := 110;
      HeightAmazon := CurrForm.AmazonSettings.HEIGHT();
      HeightEbay := CurrForm.EbaySettings.HEIGHT();
      HeightMarketplaceType := CurrForm."Marketplace Type".HEIGHT();
      MarketplaceVisible := ("Shop Typ" = "Shop Typ"::marketplace);
      CurrForm."Marketplace Type".ENABLED(MarketplaceVisible);

      AmazonFieldsVisible := ("Marketplace Type" = "Marketplace Type"::Amazon);
      EbayFieldsVisible := ("Marketplace Type" = "Marketplace Type"::eBay);


      CurrForm.AmazonSettings.VISIBLE(FALSE);
      CurrForm.EbaySettings.VISIBLE(FALSE);

      IF MarketplaceVisible THEN BEGIN
        //Amazon
        CurrForm.AmazonSettings.VISIBLE(AmazonFieldsVisible);
        CurrForm.AmazonSettings.YPOS(YPos + Spacer + HeightMarketplaceType + Spacer);

        //Ebay
        CurrForm.EbaySettings.VISIBLE(EbayFieldsVisible);
        CurrForm.EbaySettings.YPOS(YPos + Spacer + HeightMarketplaceType + Spacer);
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CheckUseOnlineOrderNo@1000000000();
    BEGIN
      UseOnlineOrderNoPossible := FALSE;
      UseOnlineOrderNoError := '';
      SalesSetup.GET;

      IF "Weborder Typ" = "Weborder Typ"::Quote THEN BEGIN
        IF "Quote No. Series Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF NoSeries.GET("Quote No. Series Code") THEN BEGIN
            IF NoSeries."Manual Nos." THEN
              UseOnlineOrderNoPossible := TRUE
            ELSE
              UseOnlineOrderNoError := STRSUBSTNO(Text001,FORMAT("Quote No. Series Code"));
          END ELSE
            UseOnlineOrderNoError := STRSUBSTNO(Text002,FORMAT("Quote No. Series Code"));
        END ELSE BEGIN
          NoSeries.SETRANGE(NoSeries.Code,SalesSetup."Quote Nos.");
          IF NoSeries.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            IF NoSeries."Manual Nos." THEN
              UseOnlineOrderNoPossible := TRUE
            ELSE
              UseOnlineOrderNoError := STRSUBSTNO(Text003,FORMAT(SalesSetup."Quote Nos."));
          END ELSE
            UseOnlineOrderNoError := Text004;
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF "Order No. Series Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF NoSeries.GET("Order No. Series Code") THEN BEGIN
            IF NoSeries."Manual Nos." THEN
              UseOnlineOrderNoPossible := TRUE
            ELSE
              UseOnlineOrderNoError := STRSUBSTNO(Text001,FORMAT("Order No. Series Code"));
          END ELSE
            UseOnlineOrderNoError := STRSUBSTNO(Text002,FORMAT("Order No. Series Code"));
        END ELSE BEGIN
          NoSeries.SETRANGE(NoSeries.Code,SalesSetup."Order Nos.");
          IF NoSeries.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            IF NoSeries."Manual Nos." THEN
              UseOnlineOrderNoPossible := TRUE
            ELSE
              UseOnlineOrderNoError := STRSUBSTNO(Text003,FORMAT(SalesSetup."Order Nos."));
          END ELSE
            UseOnlineOrderNoError := Text004;
        END;
      END;

      IF (NOT (UseOnlineOrderNoPossible)) AND ("Use Online Order No.") THEN
        ERROR(UseOnlineOrderNoError);
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- MysydeShop - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

                                                   { ID=1000000090;
                                                     CaptionML=[DEU=Marktplatz: Unterkategorien abhol.;
                                                                ENU=Marketplace: Get Subcategories];
                                                     OnPush=VAR
                                                              ParentCodeLineNo@1000000005 : Integer;
                                                              Category@1000000004 : Record 5447303;
                                                              Window@1000000003 : Dialog;
                                                              Factor@1000000002 : Decimal;
                                                              Counter@1000000001 : Decimal;
                                                              ExitLoop@1000000000 : Boolean;
                                                            BEGIN
                                                              IF Shop.GET("Shop Code") THEN
                                                                WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateShop(Shop);

                                                              IF Language.GET("Shop Code","Language Code") THEN
                                                                WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateLanguage(Language);

                                                              Factor := 9999 / 100;
                                                              ExitLoop := FALSE;
                                                              Counter := 0;
                                                              WebshopMarketplaceManagement.QueueMktplSubCategoriesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type","Search Code");
                                                              Window.OPEN(
                                                                '#1#################################\\' +
                                                                '@2@@@@@@@@');
                                                              Window.UPDATE(1, Text008);

                                                              REPEAT
                                                                Counter += 100;
                                                                Window.UPDATE(2, Counter);
                                                                IF Counter = 10000 THEN BEGIN
                                                                  Counter := 0;
                                                                  Window.UPDATE(2, Counter);
                                                                END;
                                                                ExitLoop := WebshopMarketplaceManagement.CheckMktplSubCategoriesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type",
                                                                "Search Code");
                                                              UNTIL ExitLoop = TRUE;
                                                              Window.CLOSE;

                                                              ParentCodeLineNo := Category.GetLineNoForSearchCode("Shop Code","Language Code","Search Code");
                                                              WebshopMarketplaceManagement.StateIndicatorOpen(Text009);
                                                              WebshopMarketplaceManagement.GetAllFetchedMktplCategories("Shop Code","Language Code",TRUE,0);
                                                              WebshopMarketplaceManagement.GetAllFetchedMktplCategories("Shop Code","Language Code",FALSE,ParentCodeLineNo);
                                                              WebshopMarketplaceManagement.StateIndicatorClose();

                                                              CurrForm.UPDATE(FALSE);
                                                            END;

                                                              EntryId@1000000000 : Integer;
                                                            BEGIN
                                                              IF Shop.GET("Shop Code") THEN
                                                                WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateShop(Shop);

                                                              IF Language.GET("Shop Code","Language Code") THEN
                                                                WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateLanguage(Language);

                                                              Factor := 9999 / 100;
                                                              ExitLoop := FALSE;

                                                              IF Shop."Marketplace Type" = Shop."Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN
                                                                EntryId := WebshopMarketplaceManagement.QueueMktplAttributesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type",
                                                                "Marketplace Root Category Code")
                                                              ELSE IF Shop."Marketplace Type" = Shop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN
                                                                EntryId := WebshopMarketplaceManagement.QueueMktplAttributesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type",

                                                                  "Marketplace Root Category Code",EntryId)
                                                                ELSE IF Shop."Marketplace Type" = Shop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN
                                                                  ExitLoop := WebshopMarketplaceManagement.CheckMktplAttributesFetch("Shop Code","Language Code",Shop."Marketplace Type",

  CODE
  {
    VAR
      IsShopMarketplace@1000000012 : Boolean;
      WebshopWebforms@1000000011 : Codeunit 5447306;
      Type@1000000010 : 'Description1,Promotion,Description2,Excerpt';
      WebformType@1000000009 : 'AttributeIcon,CouponBackground,CategoryImage,CategoryDesc,ItemDesc,ItemFile,TextModul,Placeholder,TxtModAttac,CampaignImages,Collection,ShippingOption,PaymentOption,ShippingOptionIcon,PaymentOptionIcon,Marketplace';
      WebshopShop@1000000008 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopMarketplaceManagement@1000000007 : Codeunit 5447312;
      Shop@1000000000 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000005 : Record 5447302;
      WebshopSetup@1000000001 : Record 5447300;
      WebshopManagement@1000000002 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000006 : Codeunit 5447301;
      ShopFilter@1000000003 : Code[10];
      LanguageFilter@1000000004 : Code[10];
      Text008@1000000015 : TextConst 'DEU=Anfrage wird verarbeitet...;ENU=Request is processed';
      Text009@1000000014 : TextConst 'DEU=Kategorien werden abgeholt;ENU=Categories are being fetched';
      Text010@1000000013 : TextConst 'DEU=Merkmale werden abgeholt;ENU=Attributes are being fetched';

    PROCEDURE SwapCategory@1000000004(ToCategory@1000000001 : Record 5447303);
    VAR
      TempCategory@1000000008 : Record 5447303;
      TempItemCategory@1000000006 : Record 5447305;
      SwapToCategory@1000000005 : TEMPORARY Record 5447303;
      SwapFromCategory@1000000004 : TEMPORARY Record 5447303;
      SwapToItemCategory@1000000003 : TEMPORARY Record 5447305;
      SwapFromItemCategory@1000000002 : TEMPORARY Record 5447305;
      SwapToLineNo@1000000000 : Integer;
      SwapFromLineNo@1000000007 : Integer;
      WebshopItem@1000000009 : Record 5447304;
      SwapFromWebshopItem@1000000010 : TEMPORARY Record 5447304;
      SwapToWebshopItem@1000000011 : TEMPORARY Record 5447304;
      AttributeLink@1000000012 : Record 5447321;
      SwapFromAttributeLink@1000000013 : TEMPORARY Record 5447321;
      SwapToAttributeLink@1000000014 : TEMPORARY Record 5447321;
    BEGIN

      // Kategorien nach oben oder unter verschieben

      SwapFromLineNo := "Line No.";
      SwapToLineNo := ToCategory."Line No.";

      //Artikel-Hauptkategorien �ndern
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Main Category Line No.",SwapFromLineNo);
      IF WebshopItem.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        SwapFromWebshopItem.INIT;
        SwapFromWebshopItem.TRANSFERFIELDS(WebshopItem);
        SwapFromWebshopItem."Main Category Line No." := SwapToLineNo;
        SwapFromWebshopItem.INSERT(FALSE);
      UNTIL WebshopItem.NEXT = 0;

      WebshopItem.INIT;
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Main Category Line No.",SwapToLineNo);
      IF WebshopItem.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        SwapToWebshopItem.INIT;
        SwapToWebshopItem.TRANSFERFIELDS(WebshopItem);
        SwapToWebshopItem."Main Category Line No." := SwapFromLineNo;
        SwapToWebshopItem.INSERT(FALSE);
      UNTIL WebshopItem.NEXT = 0;

      IF SwapFromWebshopItem.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
      IF WebshopItem.GET(SwapFromWebshopItem."Shop Code",SwapFromWebshopItem."Language Code",SwapFromWebshopItem."Item No.") THEN BEGIN
        WebshopItem."Main Category Line No." := SwapFromWebshopItem."Main Category Line No.";
        WebshopItem.MODIFY(TRUE);
      END;
      UNTIL SwapFromWebshopItem.NEXT = 0;

      IF SwapToWebshopItem.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
      IF WebshopItem.GET(SwapToWebshopItem."Shop Code",SwapToWebshopItem."Language Code",SwapToWebshopItem."Item No.") THEN BEGIN
        WebshopItem."Main Category Line No." := SwapToWebshopItem."Main Category Line No.";
        WebshopItem.MODIFY(TRUE);
      END;
      UNTIL SwapToWebshopItem.NEXT = 0;


      // Kateogorie-Attribute �ber temp. Tabellen verschieben
      AttributeLink.RESET;
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Category);
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Category Line No.",SwapFromLineNo);
      IF AttributeLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        SwapFromAttributeLink.INIT;
        SwapFromAttributeLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(AttributeLink);
        SwapFromAttributeLink."Category Line No." := SwapToLineNo;
        SwapFromAttributeLink.INSERT;
      UNTIL AttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

      AttributeLink.RESET;
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Category);
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Category Line No.",SwapToLineNo);
      IF AttributeLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        SwapToAttributeLink.INIT;
        SwapToAttributeLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(AttributeLink);
        SwapToAttributeLink."Category Line No." := SwapFromLineNo;
        SwapToAttributeLink.INSERT;
      UNTIL AttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

      AttributeLink.RESET;
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Category);
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
      AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
      AttributeLink.SETFILTER("Category Line No.",'%1|%2',SwapFromLineNo,SwapToLineNo);
      AttributeLink.DELETEALL(FALSE);

      AttributeLink.RESET;
      IF SwapFromAttributeLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        AttributeLink.INIT;
        AttributeLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapFromAttributeLink);
        AttributeLink.INSERT;
      UNTIL SwapFromAttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

      AttributeLink.RESET;
      IF SwapToAttributeLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        AttributeLink.INIT;
        AttributeLink.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapToAttributeLink);
        AttributeLink.INSERT;
      UNTIL SwapToAttributeLink.NEXT = 0;


      // Gew�hlte Kategorie in tempor�re Tabelle schreiben und l�schen
      SwapFromCategory.INIT;
      SwapFromCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(Rec);
      SwapFromCategory."Line No." := SwapToLineNo;
      SwapFromCategory.INSERT(FALSE);

      IF Shop.GET("Shop Code") THEN BEGIN

        IF Shop."Use Items from Shop Code" <> '' THEN
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code",Shop."Use Items from Shop Code")
        ELSE
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code","Shop Code");

        IF Shop."Use Categorys from Shop Code" <> '' THEN
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Shop Code",Shop."Use Categorys from Shop Code")
        ELSE
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Shop Code","Shop Code");

        TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Language Code","Language Code");
        TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Language Code","Language Code");
        TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Line No.",SwapFromLineNo);
        IF TempItemCategory.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          SwapFromItemCategory.INIT;
          SwapFromItemCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(TempItemCategory);
          SwapFromItemCategory."Category Line No." := SwapToLineNo;
          SwapFromItemCategory.INSERT(FALSE);
          TempItemCategory.DELETE(FALSE);
        UNTIL TempItemCategory.NEXT = 0;
        DELETE(FALSE);

        // Zu tauschende Kategorie in tempor�re Tabelle schreiben und l�schen
        SwapToCategory.INIT;
        SwapToCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(ToCategory);
        SwapToCategory."Line No." := SwapFromLineNo;
        SwapToCategory.INSERT(FALSE);

        IF Shop."Use Items from Shop Code" <> '' THEN
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code",Shop."Use Items from Shop Code")
        ELSE
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code","Shop Code");

        IF Shop."Use Categorys from Shop Code" <> '' THEN
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Shop Code",Shop."Use Categorys from Shop Code")
        ELSE
          TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Shop Code","Shop Code");


        TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Language Code","Language Code");
        TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Language Code","Language Code");
        TempItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Line No.",SwapToLineNo);
        IF TempItemCategory.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          SwapToItemCategory.INIT;
          SwapToItemCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(TempItemCategory);
          SwapToItemCategory."Category Line No." := SwapFromLineNo;
          SwapToItemCategory.INSERT(FALSE);
          TempItemCategory.DELETE(FALSE);
        UNTIL TempItemCategory.NEXT = 0;
        ToCategory.DELETE(FALSE);

        // Gew�hlte Kategorie mit neuer Zeilennr. anlegen
        TempCategory.INIT;
        TempCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapFromCategory);
        TempCategory.INSERT(FALSE);
        IF SwapFromItemCategory.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          TempItemCategory.INIT;
          TempItemCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapFromItemCategory);
          TempItemCategory.INSERT(FALSE);
        UNTIL SwapFromItemCategory.NEXT = 0;

        // Zu tauschende Kategorie mit neuer Zeilennr. anlegen
        TempCategory.INIT;
        TempCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapToCategory);
        TempCategory.INSERT(FALSE);
        IF SwapToItemCategory.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          TempItemCategory.INIT;
          TempItemCategory.TRANSFERFIELDS(SwapToItemCategory);
          TempItemCategory.INSERT(FALSE);
        UNTIL SwapToItemCategory.NEXT = 0;

        // Datens�tze im Web tauschen
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SwapCategory("Shop Code","Language Code",SwapFromLineNo,SwapToLineNo);

        //Berechtigungen NAV und WEB tauschen
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SwapCategoryPermissions("Shop Code","Language Code",SwapFromLineNo,SwapToLineNo);

        COMMIT;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetWebshopFilter@1000000000();
    BEGIN
      // Shopcode ermitteln
      IF NOT Shop.GET(ShopFilter) THEN BEGIN
          ShopFilter := '';
        IF GETFILTER("Shop Code") <> '' THEN
          IF Shop.GET(GETFILTER("Shop Code")) THEN
            ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
        IF (ShopFilter = '') AND (WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code" <> '') THEN
          IF Shop.GET(WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code") THEN
            ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
        IF (ShopFilter = '') THEN
          IF Shop.FINDFIRST THEN
            ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
      END;

      // Sprachcode ermitteln
      IF ShopFilter <> '' THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT Language.GET(ShopFilter,LanguageFilter) THEN BEGIN
          LanguageFilter := '';
          Language.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
          IF GETFILTER("Language Code") <> '' THEN
            IF Language.GET(ShopFilter,GETFILTER("Language Code")) THEN
              LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
          IF (LanguageFilter = '') AND (Shop."Default Language Code" <> '') THEN
            IF Language.GET(ShopFilter,Shop."Default Language Code") THEN
              LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
          IF (LanguageFilter = '') THEN
            IF Language.FINDFIRST THEN
              LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
        END;

        // Filter setzten
        IF LanguageFilter <> '' THEN BEGIN
          FILTERGROUP(4);
          SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
          SETRANGE("Language Code",LanguageFilter);
          FILTERGROUP(0);
          CurrForm.UPDATE(FALSE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- MysydeShop - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

    { 1000000001;TextBox;3630 ;220  ;2750 ;440  ;CaptionML=DEU=Webshop;
                                                 SourceExpr=ShopFilter;
                                                 TableRelation="Webshop Shop".Code;
                                                 OnLookup=VAR
                                                            ShopList@1000000000 : Form 5447302;
                                                            TempShop@1000000001 : Record 5447301;
                                                          BEGIN
                                                            IF ShopFilter <> '' THEN
                                                              TempShop.GET(ShopFilter);
                                                            ShopList.SETTABLEVIEW(TempShop);
                                                            ShopList.SETRECORD(TempShop);
                                                            ShopList.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
                                                            IF ShopList.RUNMODAL = ACTION::LookupOK THEN BEGIN
                                                              ShopList.GETRECORD(TempShop);
                                                              ShopFilter := TempShop.Code;
                                                              SetWebshopFilter;
                                                            END;
                                                          END;

                                                 OnAfterValidate=BEGIN
                                                                   SetWebshopFilter
                                                                 END;
                                                                  }
    { 1000000002;Label  ;220  ;220  ;3300 ;440  ;ParentControl=1000000001 }

  CODE
  {
    VAR
      WebshopItemFile@1000000000 : Record 5447307;
      WebshopItemLink@1000000001 : Record 5447308;
      WebshopItemPicture@1000000002 : Record 5447307;
      WebshopManagement@1000000003 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000004 : Codeunit 5447301;
      ShopFilter@1000000005 : Code[10];
      LanguageFilter@1000000006 : Code[10];
      WebshopSetup@1000000007 : Record 5447300;
      Shop@1000000008 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000009 : Record 5447302;
      Text001@1000000010 : TextConst 'DEU=Artikel %1 online aktualisiert';

    PROCEDURE SetWebshopFilter@1000000002();
    VAR
      ItemNo@1000000000 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      // Shop ermitteln
      IF NOT Shop.GET(ShopFilter) THEN BEGIN
        ShopFilter := '';
        IF GETFILTER("Shop Code") <> '' THEN
          IF Shop.GET(GETFILTER("Shop Code")) THEN
            ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
        IF (ShopFilter = '') AND (WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code" <> '') THEN
          IF Shop.GET(WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code") THEN
            ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
        IF ShopFilter = '' THEN
          IF Shop.FINDFIRST THEN
            ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
      END;

      // Sprache ermitteln
      IF ShopFilter <> '' THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT Language.GET(ShopFilter,LanguageFilter) THEN BEGIN
          LanguageFilter := '';
          Language.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
          IF GETFILTER("Language Code") <> '' THEN
            IF Language.GET(ShopFilter,GETFILTER("Language Code")) THEN
              LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
          IF (LanguageFilter = '') AND (Shop."Default Language Code" <> '') THEN
            IF Language.GET(ShopFilter,Shop."Default Language Code") THEN
              LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
          IF LanguageFilter = '' THEN
            IF Language.FINDFIRST THEN
              LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
        END;

        // Filter f�r Shop und Sprache setzten
        IF LanguageFilter <> '' THEN BEGIN
          FILTERGROUP(4);
          SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
          SETRANGE("Language Code",LanguageFilter);
          FILTERGROUP(0);

          // Datensatz anlegen falls nicht vorhanden
          IF "Item No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
            ItemNo := "Item No."
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF GETFILTER("Item No.") <> '' THEN
              ItemNo := GETFILTER("Item No.");
          END;
          IF ItemNo <> '' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT GET(ShopFilter,LanguageFilter,ItemNo) THEN BEGIN
              INIT;
              VALIDATE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
              VALIDATE("Language Code",LanguageFilter);
              VALIDATE("Item No.",ItemNo);
              INSERT(TRUE);
            END;
          END;
          CurrForm.UPDATE(FALSE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE EnableShowAvailable@4();
    VAR
      WebshopShop@1000 : Record 5447301;
    BEGIN
      CurrForm.AvailableDefaultAuto.VISIBLE(FALSE);
      CurrForm.AvailableDefaultYes.VISIBLE(FALSE);
      CurrForm.AvailableDefaultNo.VISIBLE(FALSE);
      CurrForm.AvailableDefaultAuto.YPOS := CurrForm.AvailableDefaultYes.YPOS;
      CurrForm.AvailableDefaultNo.YPOS := CurrForm.AvailableDefaultYes.YPOS;

      IF WebshopShop.GET("Shop Code") THEN BEGIN
        CASE WebshopShop."Items Always Available" OF
          WebshopShop."Items Always Available"::Default:
            BEGIN
              CurrForm.AvailableDefaultAuto.VISIBLE(TRUE);
              CurrForm.AvailableDefaultYes.VISIBLE(FALSE);
              CurrForm.AvailableDefaultNo.VISIBLE(FALSE);
            END;
          WebshopShop."Items Always Available"::Yes:
            BEGIN
              CurrForm.AvailableDefaultAuto.VISIBLE(FALSE);
              CurrForm.AvailableDefaultYes.VISIBLE(TRUE);
              CurrForm.AvailableDefaultNo.VISIBLE(FALSE);
            END;
          WebshopShop."Items Always Available"::No:
            BEGIN
              CurrForm.AvailableDefaultAuto.VISIBLE(FALSE);
              CurrForm.AvailableDefaultYes.VISIBLE(FALSE);
              CurrForm.AvailableDefaultNo.VISIBLE(TRUE);
            END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetWeight@1000000000() Weight : Decimal;
    VAR
      Item@1000000000 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      Weight := 0;
      IF Item.GET("Item No.") THEN
        Weight := Item."Net Weight";
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- MysydeShop - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

    CaptionML=[DEU=Webshop Artikelkategorie;
               ENU=Webshop Item Category];
    TableBoxID=1000000000;
    SourceTable=Table5447305;
    OnOpenForm=BEGIN
                 IF (GETFILTER("Item Shop Code") <> '') AND (GETFILTER("Item Language Code") <> '') AND (GETFILTER("Item No.") <> '') THEN BEGIN
                   WebshopItem.SETFILTER("Shop Code",GETFILTER("Item Shop Code"));
                   WebshopItem.SETFILTER("Language Code",GETFILTER("Item Language Code"));
                   WebshopItem.SETFILTER("Item No.",GETFILTER("Item No."));
                   IF WebshopItem.COUNT = 1 THEN BEGIN
                     CurrForm."Item No.".VISIBLE := FALSE;
                     CurrForm."Item Description".VISIBLE := FALSE;
                     CurrForm."Category Line No.".VISIBLE := TRUE;
                     CurrForm."Category Name".VISIBLE := TRUE;
                     CurrForm.MoveUp.VISIBLE := FALSE;
                     CurrForm.MoveDown.VISIBLE := FALSE;
                   END;
                 END;

                 IF (GETFILTER("Category Shop Code") <> '') AND (GETFILTER("Category Language Code") <> '') AND
                   (GETFILTER("Category Line No.") <> '') THEN BEGIN
                   WebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Shop Code",GETFILTER("Category Shop Code"));
                   WebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Language Code",GETFILTER("Category Language Code"));
                   WebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",GETFILTER("Category Line No."));
                   IF WebshopCategory.COUNT = 1 THEN BEGIN
                     SETCURRENTKEY("Item Shop Code","Item Language Code","Category Shop Code","Category Language Code","Category Line No.",Sorting)
                 ;
                     CurrForm."Category Line No.".VISIBLE := FALSE;
                     CurrForm."Category Name".VISIBLE := FALSE;
                     CurrForm."Item No.".VISIBLE := TRUE;
                     CurrForm."Item Description".VISIBLE := TRUE;
                     CurrForm.MoveUp.VISIBLE := TRUE;
                     CurrForm.MoveDown.VISIBLE := TRUE;
                   END;
                 END;
               END;

                                                                  IF (Shop."Use Categorys from Shop Code" <> '') AND (Shop."Use Categorys from Shop Code" <> Shop.Code) THEN
                                                                    ShopCode := Shop."Use Categorys from Shop Code";
                                                                  IF NOT WebshopLanguage.GET(ShopCode,LanguageCode) THEN
                                                                    ERROR(Text001,ShopCode,LanguageCode)
                                                                  ELSE BEGIN
                                                                    "Category Shop Code" := ShopCode;
                                                                    "Category Language Code" := LanguageCode;
                                                                  END;
                                                                END;
                                                              END;

                                                 OnLookup=BEGIN
                                                            CLEAR(WebshopCategoryList);
                                                            WebshopCategory.RESET;
                                                            ShopCode := GETFILTER("Item Shop Code");
                                                            LanguageCode := GETFILTER("Item Language Code");
                                                            IF (ShopCode <> '') AND (LanguageCode <> '') THEN BEGIN
                                                              ShopFilter := ShopCode;
                                                              Shop.SETFILTER(Code,'<>%1',ShopCode);
                                                              Shop.SETRANGE("Use Items from Shop Code",ShopCode);
                                                              IF Shop.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                                                                ShopFilter := ShopFilter + '|' + Shop.Code;
                                                              UNTIL Shop.NEXT = 0;
                                                              WebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
                                                              WebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code",LanguageCode);
                                                              WebshopCategoryList.SETTABLEVIEW(WebshopCategory);
                                                              WebshopCategoryList.SETRECORD(WebshopCategory);
                                                              WebshopCategoryList.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
                                                              IF WebshopCategoryList.RUNMODAL = ACTION::LookupOK THEN BEGIN
                                                                WebshopCategoryList.GETRECORD(WebshopCategory);
                                                                "Category Shop Code" := WebshopCategory."Shop Code";
                                                                "Category Language Code" := WebshopCategory."Language Code";
                                                                "Category Line No." := WebshopCategory."Line No.";
                                                              END;
                                                            END;
                                                          END;

  CODE
  {
    VAR
      WebshopManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000003 : Codeunit 5447301;
      WebshopWebforms@1000000001 : Codeunit 5447306;
      Type@1000000004 : 'Description1,Promotion,Description2';
      WebformType@1000000002 : 'AttributeIcon,CouponBackground,CategoryImage,CategoryDesc,ItemDesc,ItemFile,TextModul,Placeholder,TxtModAttac,CampaignImages,Collection';

    PROCEDURE EditContent@1000000000();
    VAR
      WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304;
    BEGIN
      // Beschreibung in Webform bearbeiten
      TESTFIELD("Line No.");

      //Webshopartikel aktualisieren (f�r intelligente Suche)
      WebshopItem.GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.");
      IF ((WebshopItem."Last Date Modified" > WebshopItem."Last Date Updated") OR (WebshopItem."Last Date Updated" = 0DT)) THEN BEGIN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateWebshopItemManually(WebshopItem);
        GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.","Line No.");

      WebshopShop@1000000004 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopItemDescription@1000000005 : Record 5447306;

    PROCEDURE EditContent@1000000000();
    VAR
      WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304;
      Type@1000000002 : 'Description1,Promotion,Description2';
      WebformType@1000000001 : 'AttributeIcon,CouponBackground,CategoryImage,CategoryDesc,ItemDesc,ItemFile,TextModul,Placeholder,TxtModAttac,CampaignImages,Collection';
    BEGIN
      // Artikelbeschreibung in Webform bearbeiten
      IF WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest THEN BEGIN
        TESTFIELD("Line No.");
        WebshopItem.GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.");
        IF ((WebshopItem."Last Date Modified" > WebshopItem."Last Date Updated") OR (WebshopItem."Last Date Updated" = 0DT)) THEN BEGIN
          WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateWebshopItemManually(WebshopItem);
          GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.","Line No.");
        END;

        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateWebshopItemDescription(Rec);
        GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.","Line No.");
        COMMIT;

        CLEAR(WebshopWebforms);
        WebshopWebforms.SetWebformType(WebformType::ItemDesc);
        WebshopWebforms.SetShopCode("Shop Code");
        WebshopWebforms.SetLanguageCode("Language Code");
        WebshopWebforms.SetItemNo("Item No.");
        WebshopWebforms.SetLineNo("Line No.");
        WebshopWebforms.SetRecordCode(Description);
        WebshopWebforms.ShowWebform;

        GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.","Line No.");
        WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckWebshopItemDescription(Rec);
        GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.","Line No.");

      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE @1000000001();
    BEGIN
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- MysydeShop - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }

    BEGIN
      // Textbaustein in Webform bearbeiten
      IF WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest THEN BEGIN
        TESTFIELD(Code);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateTextModule(Rec);
        GET(Code);
        COMMIT;

        CLEAR(WebshopWebforms);
        WebshopWebforms.SetWebformType(WebformType::TextModul);
        WebshopWebforms.SetRecordCode(Code);
        WebshopWebforms.ShowWebform;

        GET(Code);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckTextModule(Rec);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
        GET(Code);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE EditAttContent@1000000001(AttachmentNo@1000000000 : Integer);
    BEGIN
      // Textbaustein in Webform bearbeiten
      IF WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest THEN BEGIN
        TESTFIELD(Code);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateTextModule(Rec);
        GET(Code);
        COMMIT;

        CLEAR(WebshopWebforms);
        WebshopWebforms.SetWebformType(WebformType::TxtModAttac);
        WebshopWebforms.SetRecordCode(Code);
        WebshopWebforms.SetAttachmentNo(AttachmentNo);
        WebshopWebforms.ShowWebform;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteAttContent@1000000002(AttachmentNo@1000000000 : Integer);
    BEGIN
      IF WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest THEN BEGIN
        TESTFIELD(Code);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateTextModule(Rec);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.DeleteTextModuleAttachment(Rec,1);
        GET(Code);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckTextModuleAttachment(Rec,1);
        GET(Code);
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- MysydeShop - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

      ToAttributeOption@1000000002 : Record 5447322;
      FromSorting@1000000000 : Integer;
      ToSorting@1000000001 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      CurrForm.UPDATE(FALSE);
      ToAttributeOption.SETRANGE("Attribute Code","Attribute Code");

      IF ToAttributeOption.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
        FromSorting := Sorting;
        ToSorting := ToAttributeOption.Sorting;
        Sorting := ToSorting;
        MODIFY(FALSE);
        ToAttributeOption.Sorting := FromSorting;
        ToAttributeOption.MODIFY(FALSE);
      END;
      CurrForm.UPDATE(FALSE);
    END;

    PROCEDURE MoveUp@1000000000();
    VAR
      ToAttributeOption@1000000002 : Record 5447322;
      FromSorting@1000000000 : Integer;
      ToSorting@1000000001 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      CurrForm.UPDATE(FALSE);
      ToAttributeOption.SETRANGE("Attribute Code","Attribute Code");
      ToAttributeOption.SETFILTER(Sorting,'<%1',Sorting);
      IF ToAttributeOption.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
        FromSorting := Sorting;
        ToSorting := ToAttributeOption.Sorting;
        Sorting := ToSorting;
        MODIFY(FALSE);
        ToAttributeOption.Sorting := FromSorting;
        ToAttributeOption.MODIFY(FALSE);
      END;
      CurrForm.UPDATE(FALSE);
    END;

    PROCEDURE MoveDown@1000000001();
    VAR
      ToAttributeOption@1000000002 : Record 5447322;
      FromSorting@1000000000 : Integer;
      ToSorting@1000000001 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      CurrForm.UPDATE(FALSE);
      ToAttributeOption.SETRANGE("Attribute Code","Attribute Code");
      ToAttributeOption.SETFILTER(Sorting,'>%1',Sorting);
      IF ToAttributeOption.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        FromSorting := Sorting;
        ToSorting := ToAttributeOption.Sorting;
        Sorting := ToSorting;
        MODIFY(FALSE);
        ToAttributeOption.Sorting := FromSorting;
        ToAttributeOption.MODIFY(FALSE);
      END;
      CurrForm.UPDATE(FALSE);
    END;

    PROCEDURE EditContent@1000000002();
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest();
      CurrForm.UPDATE;
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAttributeOption(Rec);
      CurrForm.UPDATE;
      COMMIT;

      CLEAR(WebshopWebforms);
      WebshopWebforms.SetWebformType(WebformType::AttributeIcon);
      WebshopWebforms.SetRecordCode(Code);
      WebshopWebforms.SetAttributeCode("Attribute Code");
      WebshopWebforms.ShowWebform;

      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckFilterImage(Rec);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
      CurrForm.UPDATE;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- MysydeShop - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.

                                                            LanguageList@1000000000 : Form 5447304;
                                                            TempLanguage@1000000001 : Record 5447302;
                                                          BEGIN
                                                            IF ShopFilter <> '' THEN BEGIN
                                                              TempLanguage.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);

                                                                TempLanguage.GET(ShopFilter,LanguageFilter);

                                                                LanguageFilter := TempLanguage.Code;

                                                 SourceExpr=ShopFilter;
                                                 TableRelation="Webshop Shop".Code;
                                                 OnLookup=VAR
                                                            ShopList@1000000000 : Form 5447302;
                                                            TempShop@1000000001 : Record 5447301;
                                                          BEGIN

                                                              TempShop.GET(ShopFilter);
                                                            ShopList.SETTABLEVIEW(TempShop);
                                                            ShopList.SETRECORD(TempShop);
                                                            ShopList.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
                                                            IF ShopList.RUNMODAL = ACTION::LookupOK THEN BEGIN
                                                              ShopList.GETRECORD(TempShop);
                                                              ShopFilter := TempShop.Code;
                                                              SetWebshopFilter;
                                                            END;

                                                                 END;

  CODE
  {
    VAR
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447301;
      Text001@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU=Gutschein aktualisiert';
      Text002@1000000002 : TextConst 'DEU=Gutschein gebucht';
      ShopFilter@1000000007 : Code[10];
      LanguageFilter@1000000006 : Code[10];
      WebshopSetup@1000000005 : Record 5447300;
      Shop@1000000004 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000003 : Record 5447302;
      CouponHeader@1000000008 : Record 5447330;

    PROCEDURE SetWebshopFilter@1000000002();
    VAR
      Code@1000000000 : Code[10];
    BEGIN
      // Shop ermitteln
      IF NOT Shop.GET(ShopFilter) THEN BEGIN
        ShopFilter := '';
        IF GETFILTER("Shop Code") <> '' THEN
          IF Shop.GET(GETFILTER("Shop Code")) THEN
            ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
        IF (ShopFilter = '') AND (WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code" <> '') THEN
          IF Shop.GET(WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code") THEN
            ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
        IF ShopFilter = '' THEN
          IF Shop.FINDFIRST THEN
            ShopFilter := Shop.Code;
      END;

      // Sprache ermitteln
      IF ShopFilter <> '' THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT Language.GET(ShopFilter,LanguageFilter) THEN BEGIN
          LanguageFilter := '';
          Language.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
          IF GETFILTER("Language Code") <> '' THEN
            IF Language.GET(ShopFilter,GETFILTER("Language Code")) THEN
              LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
          IF (LanguageFilter = '') AND (Shop."Default Language Code" <> '') THEN
            IF Language.GET(ShopFilter,Shop."Default Language Code") THEN
              LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
          IF LanguageFilter = '' THEN
            IF Language.FINDFIRST THEN
              LanguageFilter := Language.Code;
        END;

        // Filter f�r Shop und Sprache setzten
        IF LanguageFilter <> '' THEN BEGIN
          FILTERGROUP(4);
          SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
          SETRANGE("Language Code",LanguageFilter);
          FILTERGROUP(0);

          // Datensatz anlegen falls nicht vorhanden
          IF Code <> '' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT GET(ShopFilter,LanguageFilter,Code) THEN BEGIN
              INIT;
              VALIDATE("Shop Code",ShopFilter);
              VALIDATE("Language Code",LanguageFilter);
              VALIDATE(Code,Code);
              INSERT(TRUE);
            END;
          END;
          CurrForm.UPDATE(FALSE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- MysydeShop - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

             CurrForm."Amount Left".VISIBLE := TRUE;
             CurrForm."Max No of Usage".VISIBLE := TRUE;

           END;

                     Posted := FALSE;

    { 1000000010;Label  ;0    ;0    ;0    ;0    ;ParentControl=1000000009;

                                                 ParentControl=1000000000;

                                                 InColumnHeading=Yes }

  {
    VAR
      Blank@1000000000 : Text[30];
      WebshopManagement@1000000001 : Codeunit 5447300;

    PROCEDURE FormatFields@1000000000(CouponHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447330);
    BEGIN
      CASE CouponHeader."Value Type" OF
        0:
          BEGIN
            CurrForm."Customer No".VISIBLE := FALSE;
            CurrForm.Amount.VISIBLE := FALSE;
            CurrForm."Amount Left".VISIBLE := FALSE;
            CurrForm."Max No of Usage".VISIBLE := TRUE;
          END;
        1:
          BEGIN
            CurrForm."Customer No".VISIBLE := FALSE;
            CurrForm.Amount.VISIBLE := FALSE;
            CurrForm."Amount Left".VISIBLE := FALSE;
            CurrForm."Max No of Usage".VISIBLE := TRUE;
          END;
        2:
          BEGIN
            CurrForm."Customer No".VISIBLE := FALSE;
            CurrForm.Amount.VISIBLE := FALSE;
            CurrForm."Amount Left".VISIBLE := FALSE;
            CurrForm."Max No of Usage".VISIBLE := TRUE;
          END;
        3:
          BEGIN
            CurrForm."Customer No".VISIBLE := TRUE;
            CurrForm.Amount.VISIBLE := TRUE;
            CurrForm."Amount Left".VISIBLE := TRUE;
            IF CouponHeader."Value Coupon" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              CurrForm."Max No of Usage".VISIBLE := FALSE;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              CurrForm."Max No of Usage".VISIBLE := TRUE;
            END;
          END;
        4:
          BEGIN
            CurrForm."Customer No".VISIBLE := FALSE;
            CurrForm.Amount.VISIBLE := FALSE;
            CurrForm."Amount Left".VISIBLE := FALSE;
            CurrForm."Max No of Usage".VISIBLE := TRUE;
          END;
      END;
      CurrForm.UPDATE;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetValues@1000000002(CouponHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447330);
    VAR
      CouponLine@1000000001 : Record 5447329;
    BEGIN
      CouponLine.SETRANGE(Code,CouponHeader.Code);
      IF CouponLine.FINDFIRST THEN REPEAT
        CouponLine."Max No of Usage" := 0;
        CouponLine.MODIFY;
        COMMIT;
      UNTIL CouponLine.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- MysydeShop - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

OBJECT Form 5447337 Webshop Permissions Group Link
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=22.08.17;
    Time=16:00:00;
    Version List=MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    Width=13750;
    Height=6710;
    CaptionML=[DEU=Webshop Berechtigungsverkn�pfung;
               ENU=Webshop Permission Link];
    TableBoxID=1000000000;
    SourceTable=Table5447337;
    DelayedInsert=Yes;
    OnOpenForm=BEGIN
                 CurrForm."Customer No.".VISIBLE(FALSE);
                 CurrForm."Customer Name".VISIBLE(FALSE);
                 CurrForm."Item No.".VISIBLE(FALSE);
                 CurrForm."Item Description".VISIBLE(FALSE);
                 CurrForm."Permisson Group Code".VISIBLE(FALSE);
                 CurrForm."Permission Group Description".VISIBLE(FALSE);
                 CurrForm."Shop Code".VISIBLE(FALSE);
                 CurrForm."Language Code".VISIBLE(FALSE);
                 CurrForm."Line No.".VISIBLE(FALSE);
                 CurrForm."Shipping Description".VISIBLE(FALSE);
                 CurrForm."Payment Description".VISIBLE(FALSE);

                 CASE GETFILTER(Type) OF
                   'CustomerGroup': BEGIN
                     IF GETFILTER("Customer No.") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Permisson Group Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Permission Group Description".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text001,GETFILTER("Customer No.")));
                     END ELSE IF GETFILTER("Permisson Group Code") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Customer No.".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Customer Name".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text002,GETFILTER("Permisson Group Code")));
                     END;
                   END;
                   'ItemGroup': BEGIN
                     IF GETFILTER("Item No.") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Permisson Group Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Permission Group Description".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text003,GETFILTER("Item No.")));
                     END ELSE IF GETFILTER("Permisson Group Code") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Item No.".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Item Description".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text004,GETFILTER("Permisson Group Code")));
                     END;
                   END;
                   'CustomerItem': BEGIN
                     IF GETFILTER("Customer No.") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Item No.".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Item Description".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text005,GETFILTER("Customer No.")));
                     END ELSE IF GETFILTER("Item No.") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Customer No.".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Customer Name".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text006,GETFILTER("Item No.")));
                     END;
                   END;
                   'CustomerCategory': BEGIN
                     IF GETFILTER("Customer No.") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Shop Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Language Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Line No.".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text008,GETFILTER("Customer No.")));
                     END ELSE IF GETFILTER("Line No.") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Customer No.".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Customer Name".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text007,GETFILTER("Line No.")));
                     END;
                   END;
                   'CategoryGroup': BEGIN
                     IF GETFILTER("Line No.") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Permisson Group Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Permission Group Description".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text009,GETFILTER("Line No.")));
                     END ELSE IF GETFILTER("Permisson Group Code") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Shop Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Language Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Line No.".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text010,GETFILTER("Permisson Group Code")));
                     END;
                   END;
                 'CustomerShipping': BEGIN
                     IF GETFILTER("Customer No.") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Shop Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Language Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Line No.".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text018,GETFILTER("Customer No.")));
                     END ELSE IF GETFILTER("Line No.") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Customer No.".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Customer Name".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text017,GETFILTER("Line No.")));
                     END;
                   END;
                   'ShippingGroup': BEGIN
                     IF GETFILTER("Line No.") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Permisson Group Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Permission Group Description".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text019,GETFILTER("Line No.")));
                     END ELSE IF GETFILTER("Permisson Group Code") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Shop Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Language Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Line No.".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text020,GETFILTER("Permisson Group Code")));
                     END;
                   END;
                   'CustomerPayment': BEGIN
                     IF GETFILTER("Customer No.") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Shop Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Language Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Line No.".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text024,GETFILTER("Customer No.")));
                     END ELSE IF GETFILTER("Line No.") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Customer No.".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Customer Name".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text023,GETFILTER("Line No.")));
                     END;
                   END;
                   'PaymentGroup': BEGIN
                     IF GETFILTER("Line No.") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Permisson Group Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Permission Group Description".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text021,GETFILTER("Line No.")));
                     END ELSE IF GETFILTER("Permisson Group Code") <> '' THEN BEGIN
                       CurrForm."Shop Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Language Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm."Line No.".VISIBLE(TRUE);
                       CurrForm.CAPTION(STRSUBSTNO(Text022,GETFILTER("Permisson Group Code")));
                     END;
                   END;
                 END;
               END;

  }

    PROCEDURE SetFilters@1000000000(ParamType@1000000004 : 'CustomerGroup,ItemGroup,CustomerItem,CustomerCategory,CategoryGroup';ParamCustNo@1000000000 : Code[20];ParamItemNo@1000000001 : Code[20];ParamGroupCode@1000000002 : Code[20];ParamCatLineNo@1000000003 : Integer);

        SETRANGE("Item No.",ParamItemNo);

    PROCEDURE SetTypeIsOrderOption@1000000006();
    BEGIN
      IF Type = Type::SubscriptionOrderOption THEN BEGIN
        //TypeIsSequenceItem := FALSE;
        CurrForm."Payment Type".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."Item No. Subscription Item".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."Start Type".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."No. of Sequence Steps".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."Price per Billing Interval".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."Allow Late Entry".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm.SequenceItem.EDITABLE(FALSE);

        //TypeIsOrderOption := TRUE;
        CurrForm."Start Date Formula".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."Turn Interval Formula".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."No. of Items".EDITABLE(TRUE);

        "Payment Type" := "Payment Type"::PerTurn;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        //TypeIsSequenceItem := TRUE;
        CurrForm."Payment Type".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."Item No. Subscription Item".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."Start Type".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."No. of Sequence Steps".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."Price per Billing Interval".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."Allow Late Entry".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm.SequenceItem.EDITABLE(TRUE);

        //TypeIsOrderOption := FALSE;
        CurrForm."Start Date Formula".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."Turn Interval Formula".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."No. of Items".EDITABLE(FALSE);

      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetTypeIsSequenceItem@1000000007();
    BEGIN
      IF Type = Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem THEN BEGIN
        //TypeIsOrderOption := FALSE;
        CurrForm."Start Date Formula".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."Turn Interval Formula".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."No. of Items".EDITABLE(FALSE);

        //TypeIsSequenceItem := TRUE;
        CurrForm."Payment Type".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."Item No. Subscription Item".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."Start Type".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."No. of Sequence Steps".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."Price per Billing Interval".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."Allow Late Entry".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm.SequenceItem.EDITABLE(TRUE);

      END ELSE BEGIN
        "Payment Type" := "Payment Type"::PerTurn;
        //TypeIsOrderOption := TRUE;
        CurrForm."Start Date Formula".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."Turn Interval Formula".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."No. of Items".EDITABLE(TRUE);

        //TypeIsSequenceItem := FALSE;
        CurrForm."Payment Type".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."Item No. Subscription Item".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."Start Type".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."No. of Sequence Steps".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."Price per Billing Interval".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."Allow Late Entry".EDITABLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm.SequenceItem.EDITABLE(FALSE);

      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetCanSetLateEntryPossible@1000000008();
    BEGIN
      IF (Type = Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem)
         AND ("Start Type" = "Start Type"::Join)
         AND ("Payment Type" = "Payment Type"::Once)  THEN BEGIN
        //CanSetLateEntryPossible := TRUE;
        CurrForm."Allow Late Entry".EDITABLE(TRUE);

      END ELSE BEGIN
        //CanSetLateEntryPossible := FALSE;
        CurrForm."Allow Late Entry".EDITABLE(TRUE);
        IF "Allow Late Entry" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          "Allow Late Entry" := FALSE;
          MODIFY(FALSE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    END.
  }

    VAR
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447340;

    PROCEDURE SetIsLinkedToSequenceStep@1000000001();
    BEGIN
      IF GETFILTER("Link to") = FORMAT("Link to"::"Sequence Step") THEN BEGIN
        //IsLinkedToSequenceStep := TRUE;
        CurrForm."Variant Code".VISIBLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm.Quantity.VISIBLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."Min. Quantity".VISIBLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."Max. Quantity".VISIBLE(FALSE);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        //IsLinkedToSequenceStep := FALSE;
        CurrForm."Variant Code".VISIBLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm.Quantity.VISIBLE(FALSE);
        CurrForm."Min. Quantity".VISIBLE(TRUE);
        CurrForm."Max. Quantity".VISIBLE(TRUE);

      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    END.
  }

  {
    VAR
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000002 : Codeunit 5447301;
      WebshopWebforms@1000000003 : Codeunit 5447306;
      Type@1000000001 : 'Description1,Promotion,Description2';
      WebformType@1000000000 : 'AttributeIcon,CouponBackground,CategoryImage,CategoryDesc,ItemDesc,ItemFile,TextModul,Placeholder,TxtModAttac,CampaignImages,Collection,ShippingOption,PaymentOption,ShippingOptionIcon,PaymentOptionIcon';
      Text001@1000000005 : TextConst 'DEU=Logo gel�scht;ENU=Logo deleted';
      Text002@1000000004 : TextConst 'DEU=Beschreibung gel�scht;ENU=Description deleted';

    PROCEDURE EditContent@1000000001();
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD("Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateShippingOption(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      COMMIT;

      CLEAR(WebshopWebforms);
      WebshopWebforms.SetWebformType(WebformType::ShippingOption);
      WebshopWebforms.SetShopCode("Shop Code");
      WebshopWebforms.SetLanguageCode("Language Code");
      WebshopWebforms.SetLineNo("Line No.");
      WebshopWebforms.ShowWebform;

      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckShippingOption(Rec);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
    END;

    PROCEDURE EditIcon@1000000003();
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD("Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateShippingOption(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      COMMIT;

      CLEAR(WebshopWebforms);
      WebshopWebforms.SetWebformType(WebformType::ShippingOptionIcon);
      WebshopWebforms.SetShopCode("Shop Code");
      WebshopWebforms.SetLanguageCode("Language Code");
      WebshopWebforms.SetLineNo("Line No.");
      WebshopWebforms.ShowWebform;

      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckShippingOptionIcon(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- MysydeShop - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD("Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdatePaymentOption(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");

      CLEAR(WebshopWebforms);

      WebshopWebforms.SetShopCode("Shop Code");
      WebshopWebforms.SetLanguageCode("Language Code");
      WebshopWebforms.SetLineNo("Line No.");

      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckPaymentOption(Rec);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
    END;

      WebshopWebforms.SetShopCode("Shop Code");
      WebshopWebforms.SetLanguageCode("Language Code");
      WebshopWebforms.SetLineNo("Line No.");
      WebshopWebforms.ShowWebform;

      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckPaymentOptionIcon(Rec);
      GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);

  CODE
  {
    VAR
      ItemNo@1000000001 : Code[30];
      WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304;

    PROCEDURE SetItemNo@1000000000(ParamItemNo@1000000001 : Code[30]);
    VAR
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000003 : Record 5447340;
      WebshopSubscriptionItemLink@1000000002 : Record 5447342;
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000000 : Record 5447341;
    BEGIN
      ItemNo := ParamItemNo;
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader.SETRANGE("Item No. Subscription Item",ParamItemNo);
      Rec.DELETEALL(FALSE);
      IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        Rec := WebshopSubscriptionHeader;
        Rec.INSERT(FALSE);
        CurrForm.UPDATE(FALSE);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader.RESET;
        WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.",ParamItemNo);
        IF WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.GET(WebshopSubscriptionItemLink."Subscription Code") AND
            (NOT Rec.GET(WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code)) THEN BEGIN
            Rec := WebshopSubscriptionHeader;
            Rec.INSERT(FALSE);
          END;
        UNTIL WebshopSubscriptionItemLink.NEXT = 0;
        CurrForm.UPDATE(FALSE);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetWebshopItem@1000000001(ParamWebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304);
    BEGIN
      WebshopItem := ParamWebshopItem;
      SetItemNo(WebshopItem."Item No.");
    END;

    BEGIN
    END.
  }

    PROCEDURE GetSelectionFilter@2() : Code[80];
    VAR
      Item@1000 : Record 27;
      FirstItem@1001 : Code[30];
      LastItem@1002 : Code[30];
      SelectionFilter@1003 : Code[250];
      ItemCount@1004 : Integer;
      More@1005 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      CurrForm.SETSELECTIONFILTER(Item);
      ItemCount := Item.COUNT;
      IF ItemCount > 0 THEN BEGIN
        Item.FIND('-');
        WHILE ItemCount > 0 DO BEGIN
          ItemCount := ItemCount - 1;
          Item.MARKEDONLY(FALSE);
          FirstItem := Item."No.";
          LastItem := FirstItem;
          More := (ItemCount > 0);
          WHILE More DO
            IF Item.NEXT = 0 THEN
              More := FALSE
            ELSE
              IF NOT Item.MARK THEN
                More := FALSE
              ELSE BEGIN
                LastItem := Item."No.";
                ItemCount := ItemCount - 1;
                IF ItemCount = 0 THEN
                  More := FALSE;
              END;
          IF SelectionFilter <> '' THEN
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + '|';
          IF FirstItem = LastItem THEN
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + FirstItem
          ELSE
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + FirstItem + '..' + LastItem;
          IF ItemCount > 0 THEN BEGIN
            Item.MARKEDONLY(TRUE);
            Item.NEXT;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      EXIT(SelectionFilter);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetSelection@1(VAR Item@1000 : Record 27);
    BEGIN
      CurrForm.SETSELECTIONFILTER(Item);
    END;

    BEGIN
    END.
  }

        ParamField::IconActItems:     ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(63);
        ParamField::BannerActItems:   ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(64);
        ParamField::BannerBasket:     ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(66);
      END;

      TESTFIELD(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest();
      CurrForm.UPDATE;
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateCampaignByCode(Code);
      CurrForm.UPDATE;
      COMMIT;

      CLEAR(WebshopWebforms);
      WebshopWebforms.SetWebformType(WebformType::CampaignImages);
      WebshopWebforms.SetShopCode("Shop Code");
      WebshopWebforms.SetLanguageCode("Language Code");
      WebshopWebforms.SetRecordCode(Code);
      WebshopWebforms.SetWebFieldName(ParamField);
      WebshopWebforms.ShowWebform;

      WebshopUpdateManagement.CheckCampaignImage(Rec,ThisFieldRef);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(FALSE);
      CurrForm.UPDATE;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteImage@1000000001(ParamField@1000000000 : 'IconCondItems,BannerCondItems,IconActItems,BannerActItems,BannerBasket');
    VAR
      ThisRecRef@1000000003 : RecordRef;
      ThisFieldRef@1000000002 : FieldRef;
      WebshopManagement@1000000001 : Codeunit 5447300;
    BEGIN
      ThisRecRef.GETTABLE(Rec);

      CASE ParamField OF
        ParamField::IconCondItems:    ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(60);
        ParamField::BannerCondItems:  ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(61);
        ParamField::IconActItems:     ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(63);
        ParamField::BannerActItems:   ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(64);
        ParamField::BannerBasket:     ThisFieldRef := ThisRecRef.FIELD(66);
      END;

      TESTFIELD(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopManagement.ConnectionTest();
      CurrForm.UPDATE;
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateCampaignByCode(Code);
      CurrForm.UPDATE;
      COMMIT;
      WebshopUpdateManagement.DeleteCampaignImage(Rec,ThisFieldRef);
      CurrForm.UPDATE;
    END;

    BEGIN
    END.
  }
}
